# Fixed / refuted bugs awaiting user verification

Rows moved here from [port_bugs.md](../port_bugs.md) once an agent set FIXED or REFUTED. IDs are permanent; cite as `port_bugs.md #NNN`. User verifies, sets CLOSED, and moves the row to [bugs_closed.md](bugs_closed.md) (resolution text may be trimmed there; this file keeps the full text).

| 1611 | FIXED | (MAPEDIT audit 2026-11-08, low) Start-up and argv residue (`FUN_1000_28d8` / `2872` / `247a` / `2516`): (1) `-c` beats `-m:`: 247a loads only when DS:0x54 is 0, so `-c -m:X.MP` asks for a new map; the port loads X.MP (`mapedit_init`). (2) `M` with no ':' still sets DS:0x4c (edit mode, empty name, so the load fails and the program ends); the port ignores it and opens the picker. (3) DOS skips a non-switch argument other than `?`; `mapedit_main.c` exits 2 with "Unknown argument". (4) A failed start-up load prints the load's own code, DS:0x62 = DS:0x4a4 (1 open, 2 header, 3 version, 4-6 plane read); the port always prints 1. (5) DOS stops with a code when FONTINTR 0x15, FONTTINY 0x16, CURSOR 0x17, ICONS 0x19 or WOODTILE 0x1b fails to load (raw 0x25e4-0x2707); the port runs on without them. (6) To check first: an empty @NEWNAME / @SAVEAS answer. DOS goes ahead whenever `FUN_133d_380a` returns 0 (it would make ".MP"); the port treats an empty name as a cancel (`me_name_result`). Whether `FUN_133d_258e` returns 0 for an empty field is not traced. | 2026-11-10: -c over -m, M scans for colon, stray args skipped, DS:0x4a4 load codes, DOS asset start-up order and codes (TERRAIN 0x322), empty names accepted |

Closed #19 (cannonade during the end of turn) drove the simplification; a fix must keep #19's case quiet only where DOS does. The same visible flag gates the naval loss popups: FUN_5fef_0352 shows @CARGOCAPTURE 0x1b69 (`param_3 && *0xa154`), @SHIPDAMAGE 0x1b76 and @SHIPSUNK 0x1b81 only on param_3, the 1b0e visible flag passed through 0ec0, while `units_apply_naval_loss_outcome` (units_combat.c:2133-2134) uses `show_popups && units_combat_human_involved` (every caller passes show_popups 1), so an AI-vs-AI sea fight the human can see shows nothing. `units_fort_hull_loss` (units_combat_resolve.c:995) already passes 03f6's visible flag. #1681 has the same shape for colony captures. Also @EVASIVE: 1b0e shows 0x1c0b whenever param_4 is set, but `units_resolve_naval_combat_ff_w` (src/core/units_combat_resolve.c:815) gates it on `units_combat_human_involved`, so an AI-vs-AI evasion in the human's view shows nothing (a trace of such a fight confirms the whole row). Also undocumented (only a code comment): the trade-route at-sea arm. DOS gives an orders==2 hull off a settlement tile a budget only if FUN_1427_101c (other hulls pick first) leaves units behind; the port gives that hull full room and lets it pick first. | 1762 | FIXED | (non-leaf fidelity review 2026-11-08, latent) Water region numbering in the map generator. In DOS FUN_67bf_0000 (raw 104804-104963; asm 67bf:01e7-01ef, 00c1-0102) the next row starts without resetting local_e ([bp-0xc], the running label) or bVar4 ([bp-6]), so at x = w-2 the north-merge loop joins the label carried from tile (1, y-1) with the label north of (w-2, y). `map_gen_assign_continents` (src/core/map_gen.c:1551-1611) carries in_run / run_id but at x = w-2 only sets run_id = cur and never merges with the carried run. A Python emulation matches on the four DOS plane dumps (original_saves/mapgen/DOS_PLANES_S*.bin) and differs on 17 of 40 synthetic lake maps; adding a row-start reset to the DOS emulation removes every difference. | 2026-11-09: map_gen_assign_continents uses DOS's running label carried across rows (FUN_67bf_0000 raw 104804-104963); invented east merge removed. |

| 1746 | FIXED | (non-leaf fidelity review 2026-11-08) Trade-route ships aim at the wrong High Seas tile for Europe stops. A colony on the west or Pacific side, a ship far north or south, or a foreign unit on that rim tile sends the ship to a different (often far) tile, and the crossing length is rolled from the exit x. The literal ring already exists as `units_015e_hs_course` (used by `game_apply_goto_port`). Also latent: `game_issue_goto` from `game_apply_goto_port` (:1575-1608) does not stamp +0x314b = 0x45 (col1_ai_plan, saved by col1_bridge.c:3527) or model DS:0x9456[nation]++.
